Previous works have extended assumption (a) to nonlinear spaces through the Invariant Extended Kalman Filter (IEKF), showing that it retains properties similar to those of the classical Kalman filter when the system dynamics are group-affine on a Lie group. More recently, the counterpart of assumption (b) for the same nonlinear setting was addressed in [1]. By means of the proposed Iterated Invariant Extended Kalman Filter (IterIEKF), the authors of that work demonstrated that the update step exhibits several compatibility properties of the classical linear Kalman filter. In this work, we introduce a novel open-source state estimation algorithm for legged robots based on the IterIEKF. The update step of the proposed filter relies solely on proprioceptive measurements, exploiting kinematic constraints on foot velocity during contact and base-frame velocity, making it inherently robust to environmental conditions. Through extensive numerical simulations and evaluation on real-world datasets, we demonstrate that the IterIEKF outperforms the vanilla IEKF, the SO(3)-based Kalman Filter, and its iterated variant in terms of both accuracy and consistency.

</details>


### [115] [One-Shot Cross-Geometry Skill Transfer through Part Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15455)
*Skye Thompson,Ondrej Biza,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种通过将物体分解为语义部件来提升机器人技能迁移能力的方法，利用生成形状模型实现交互点的高效转移，能够在模拟和真实环境中对多种技能和物体实现成功的一次性迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能迁移方法在处理形状陌生的物体时往往表现不佳，受组合建模改进迁移效果的启发，本文旨在提升机器人从演示中向新物体迁移技能的泛化能力。

Method: 将物体分解为其语义部件，利用数据高效的生成形状模型，将演示物体部件上的交互点迁移到新物体上，并自动构建优化目标以对齐技能相关部件上的这些点。

Result: 该方法在模拟和真实环境中均实现了对多种技能和物体的成功一次性迁移，且适用于比现有方法更广泛的物体几何形状。

Conclusion: 通过基于语义部件的分解与生成模型结合，显著提升了机器人技能迁移的泛化性和适应性，为一词学习下的技能迁移提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Given a demonstration, a robot should be able to generalize a skill to any object it encounters-but existing approaches to skill transfer often fail to adapt to objects with unfamiliar shapes. Motivated by examples of improved transfer from compositional modeling, we propose a method for improving transfer by decomposing objects into their constituent semantic parts. We leverage data-efficient generative shape models to accurately transfer interaction points from the parts of a demonstration object to a novel object. We autonomously construct an objective to optimize the alignment of those points on skill-relevant object parts. Our method generalizes to a wider range of object geometries than existing work, and achieves successful one-shot transfer for a range of skills and objects from a single demonstration, in both simulated and real environments.

</details>


### [116] [NeuroMesh: A Unified Neural Inference Framework for Decentralized Multi-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15475)
*Yang Zhou,Yash Shetye,Long Quang,Devon Super,Jesse Milzman,Manohari Goarin,Aditya Azad,Devang Sunil Dhake,Jeffery Mao,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NeuroMesh是一个多领域、跨平台、模块化的去中心化神经推理框架，用于在异构机器人上部署多机器人学习模型，支持协同感知、分散控制和任务分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于硬件异构性、通信限制以及缺乏统一的执行堆栈，在异构机器人上部署学习到的多机器人模型仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出NeuroMesh框架，采用双聚合范式进行信息融合，并通过并行架构解耦周期时间与端到端延迟；使用C++实现，结合Zenoh进行机器人间通信，支持GPU/CPU混合推理。

Result: 在包含空中和地面机器人的异构团队上验证了NeuroMesh在协作感知、分散控制和任务分配中的有效性，表现出对不同任务结构和负载大小的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: NeuroMesh能够有效支持异构多机器人系统的标准化神经推理，计划作为开源框架向社区发布。

Abstract: Deploying learned multi-robot models on heterogeneous robots remains challenging due to hardware heterogeneity, communication constraints, and the lack of a unified execution stack. This paper presents NeuroMesh, a multi-domain, cross-platform, and modular decentralized neural inference framework that standardizes observation encoding, message passing, aggregation, and task decoding in a unified pipeline. NeuroMesh combines a dual-aggregation paradigm for reduction- and broadcast-based information fusion with a parallelized architecture that decouples cycle time from end-to-end latency. Our high-performance C++ implementation leverages Zenoh for inter-robot communication and supports hybrid GPU/CPU inference. We validate NeuroMesh on a heterogeneous team of aerial and ground robots across collaborative perception, decentralized control, and task assignment, demonstrating robust operation across diverse task structures and payload sizes. We plan to release NeuroMesh as an open-source framework to the community.

</details>


### [117] [Trajectory Planning for Safe Dual Control with Active Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15507)
*Kaleb Ben Naveed,Manveer Singh,Devansh R. Agrawal,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Dual-gatekeeper的框架，用于在保证安全和任务成本预算的前提下，解决模型不确定性下的轨迹规划问题，实现了鲁棒规划与主动探索的结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型不确定条件下规划安全轨迹是一个核心挑战。现有方法要么过于保守，忽略不确定性减少的机会，要么缺乏对探索何时有益的正式考量，且难以兼顾安全性和任务性能。

Method: 提出了Dual-gatekeeper框架，将鲁棒规划与主动探索相结合，在满足安全性约束和任务成本预算的条件下进行不确定性缩减。探索仅在能被验证为不危害安全且不超预算时才被执行。提供了两种基于不同安全机制的实现方式。

Result: 在四旋翼导航和自动驾驶赛车两个案例中验证了该框架的有效性，能够在参数不确定性下平衡即时任务性能与长期不确定性降低。

Conclusion: Dual-gatekeeper为预算约束下的双控问题提供了具有安全性与预算可行性的解决方案，实现了探索与利用的 principled 权衡。

Abstract: Planning safe trajectories under model uncertainty is a fundamental challenge. Robust planning ensures safety by considering worst-case realizations, yet ignores uncertainty reduction and leads to overly conservative behavior. Actively reducing uncertainty on-the-fly during a nominal mission defines the dual control problem. Most approaches address this by adding a weighted exploration term to the cost, tuned to trade off the nominal objective and uncertainty reduction, but without formal consideration of when exploration is beneficial. Moreover, safety is enforced in some methods but not in others. We study a budget-constrained dual control problem, where uncertainty is reduced subject to safety and a mission-level cost budget that limits the allowable degradation in task performance due to exploration. In this work, we propose Dual-gatekeeper, a framework that integrates robust planning with active exploration under formal guarantees of safety and budget feasibility. The key idea is that exploration is pursued only when it provides a verifiable improvement without compromising safety or violating the budget, enabling the system to balance immediate task performance with long-term uncertainty reduction in a principled manner. We provide two implementations of the framework based on different safety mechanisms and demonstrate its performance on quadrotor navigation and autonomous car racing case studies under parametric uncertainty.

</details>


### [118] [ShapeGen: Robotic Data Generation for Category-Level Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15569)
*Yirui Wang,Xiuwei Xu,Angyuan Ma,Bingyao Yu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ShapeGen的无需模拟器、三维的数据生成方法，用于提升操作策略在真实场景中对同类物体形状变化的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中同类物体存在显著的几何多样性，传统依赖特定物体训练的操作策略难以泛化，且手动收集多样化形状数据成本高昂。

Method: ShapeGen分为两个阶段：首先构建包含功能对应点映射的空间形变模型库（Shape Library）；然后利用该库设计生成流程，仅需少量人工标注即可生成物理合理且功能正确的新颖操作演示。

Result: 实验证明，使用ShapeGen生成的数据训练的策略在真实环境中展现出更强的同类形状泛化能力。

Conclusion: ShapeGen能有效提升操作策略在未见形状上的鲁棒性和适应性，推动了类别级操作的发展。

Abstract: Manipulation policies deployed in uncontrolled real-world scenarios are faced with great in-category geometric diversity of everyday objects. In order to function robustly under such variations, policies need to work in a category-level manner, i.e. knowing how to interact with any object in a certain category, instead of only a specific one seen during training. This in-category generalizability is usually nurtured with shape-diversified training data; however, manually collecting such a corpus of data is infeasible due to the requirement of intense human labor and large collections of divergent objects at hand. In this paper, we propose ShapeGen, a data generation method that aims at generating shape-variated manipulation data in a simulator-free and 3D manner. ShapeGen decomposes the process into two stages: Shape Library curation and Function-Aware Generation. In the first stage, we train spatial warpings between shapes mapping points to points that correspond functionally, and aggregate 3D models along with the warpings into a plug-and-play Shape Library. In the second stage, we design a pipeline that, leveraging established Libraries, requires only minimal human annotation to generate physically plausible and functionally correct novel demonstrations. Experiments in the real world demonstrate the effectiveness of ShapeGen to boost policies' in-category shape generalizability. Project page: https://wangyr22.github.io/ShapeGen/.

</details>


### [119] [GaussianFlow SLAM: Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM Guided by GaussianFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15612)
*Dong-Uk Seo,Jinwoo Jeon,Eungchang Mason Lee,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GaussianFlow SLAM，利用光流作为几何感知线索来优化单目3DGS-SLAM中的场景结构和相机位姿，提升建图与跟踪的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目SLAM缺乏可靠的几何线索，导致高斯溅射在建图和跟踪中易陷入局部极小，产生结构退化和不准确问题。

Method: 引入光流对齐机制（GaussianFlow），使高斯投影运动与光流一致，并结合基于归一化误差的稠密化与剪枝模块优化地图。

Result: 在公开数据集上实现了优于当前最先进方法的渲染质量和位姿精度。

Conclusion: 光流可有效提供几何约束，显著提升单目高斯溅射SLAM的性能。

Abstract: Gaussian splatting has recently gained traction as a compelling map representation for SLAM systems, enabling dense and photo-realistic scene modeling. However, its application to monocular SLAM remains challenging due to the lack of reliable geometric cues from monocular input. Without geometric supervision, mapping or tracking could fall in local-minima, resulting in structural degeneracies and inaccuracies. To address this challenge, we propose GaussianFlow SLAM, a monocular 3DGS-SLAM that leverages optical flow as a geometry-aware cue to guide the optimization of both the scene structure and camera poses. By encouraging the projected motion of Gaussians, termed GaussianFlow, to align with the optical flow, our method introduces consistent structural cues to regularize both map reconstruction and pose estimation. Furthermore, we introduce normalized error-based densification and pruning modules to refine inactive and unstable Gaussians, thereby contributing to improved map quality and pose accuracy. Experiments conducted on public datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior rendering quality and tracking accuracy compared with state-of-the-art algorithms. The source code is available at: https://github.com/url-kaist/gaussianflow-slam.

</details>


### [120] [Factor Graph-Based Shape Estimation for Continuum Robots via Magnus Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15619)
*Lorenzo Ticozzi,Patricio A. Vela,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合低维几何变量应变（GVS）参数化与因子图框架的新方法，用于连续体机器人的形状重建，兼顾紧凑状态表示与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在参数化模型的紧凑性与Cosserat杆因子图推断的概率性之间难以兼顾，需一种兼具两者优势的形状估计方法。

Method: 在因子图框架中估计低维GVS参数的系数，引入基于Magnus展开的新型运动学因子，将应变系数与骨架姿态变量通过闭式杆几何先验关联。

Result: 在0.4米腱驱连续机器人仿真中，三种测量配置下平均位置误差低于2毫米，仅位置测量时姿态误差比高斯过程回归基线降低六倍。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了紧凑状态表示、概率推理和计算效率的统一，适用于基于模型的控制，且在稀疏噪声数据下表现优越。

Abstract: Reconstructing the shape of continuum manipulators from sparse, noisy sensor data is a challenging task, owing to the infinite-dimensional nature of such systems. Existing approaches broadly trade off between parametric methods that yield compact state representations but lack probabilistic structure, and Cosserat rod inference on factor graphs, which provides principled uncertainty quantification at the cost of a state dimension that grows with the spatial discretization. This letter combines the strength of both paradigms by estimating the coefficients of a low-dimensional Geometric Variable Strain (GVS) parameterization within a factor graph framework. A novel kinematic factor, derived from the Magnus expansion of the strain field, encodes the closed-form rod geometry as a prior constraint linking the GVS strain coefficients to the backbone pose variables. The resulting formulation yields a compact state vector directly amenable to model-based control, while retaining the modularity, probabilistic treatment and computational efficiency of factor graph inference. The proposed method is evaluated in simulation on a 0.4 m long tendon-driven continuum robot under three measurement configurations, achieving mean position errors below 2 mm for all three scenarios and demonstrating a sixfold reduction in orientation error compared to a Gaussian process regression baseline when only position measurements are available.

</details>


### [121] [Contact-Aware Planning and Control of Continuum Robots in Highly Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15638)
*Aedan Mangan,Kehan Long,Ki Myung Brian Lee,Miheer Potdar,Nikolay Atanasov,Tania K. Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种接触感知的规划方法，用于连续体机器人在狭窄解剖环境中的安全导航，通过评估接触质量避免危险接触，并在硬件实验中实现100%的目标到达率和低跟踪误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人在狭窄脆弱环境中运动时不可避免会与周围结构接触，不良接触可能降低可控性、引发运动学奇异或带来安全风险，因此需要一种能够区分并处理不同类型接触的规划方法。

Method: 提出一种接触感知的规划方法，通过评估接触质量对危险接触进行惩罚，同时允许无害接触发生；生成运动学可行的轨迹和可用于闭环控制的接触感知雅可比矩阵，并在基于患者扫描数据的解剖模型上进行系统验证。

Result: 在三种常见解剖环境中均生成有效路径，硬件实验中100%成功到达目标且避免了危险尖端接触；平均跟踪误差分别为1.9±0.5 mm、1.2±0.1 mm和1.7±0.2 mm；消融研究表明惩罚连续段末端（ECS）接触可提高操作灵活性并防止硬件故障。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了在高度受限环境中可靠、具有接触感知能力的连续体机器人导航，提升了系统安全性与可控性。

Abstract: Continuum robots are well suited for navigating confined and fragile environments, such as vascular or endoluminal anatomy, where contact with surrounding structures is often unavoidable. While controlled contact can assist motion, unfavorable contact can degrade controllability, induce kinematic singularities, or introduce safety risks. We present a contact-aware planning approach that evaluates contact quality, penalizing hazardous interactions, while permitting benign contact. The planner produces kinematically feasible trajectories and contact-aware Jacobians which can be used for closed-loop control in hardware experiments. We validate the approach by testing the integrated system (planning, control, and mechanical design) on anatomical models from patient scans. The planner generates effective plans for three common anatomical environments, and, in all hardware trials, the continuum robot was able to reach the target while avoiding dangerous tip contact (100% success). Mean tracking errors were 1.9 +/- 0.5 mm, 1.2 +/- 0.1 mm, and 1.7 +/- 0.2 mm across the three different environments. Ablation studies showed that penalizing end-of-continuum-segment (ECS) contact improved manipulability and prevented hardware failures. Overall, this work enables reliable, contact-aware navigation in highly constrained environments.

</details>


### [122] [Fuzzy Logic Theory-based Adaptive Reward Shaping for Robust Reinforcement Learning (FARS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15772)
*Hürkan Şahin,Van Huyen Dang,Erdi Sayar,Alper Yegenoglu,Erdal Kayacan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于模糊逻辑的奖励塑形方法，将专家知识融入强化学习奖励设计，提升了高维状态空间和长视野任务中的学习效率和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有高维状态空间和长视野的实际任务中，稀疏或固定的奖励会严重减缓探索速度并导致智能体陷入局部最优，因此需要更有效的奖励设计方法。

Method: 利用模糊逻辑将人类直觉编码为自适应且可解释的规则，根据智能体状态动态调整奖励贡献，实现快速运动与精确控制之间的平滑过渡。

Result: 在自主无人机竞速仿真中验证了该方法的有效性，相比非模糊奖励方法，学习更稳定，收敛更快，训练种子间性能波动更小，在较难场景下成功率最高提升约5%。

Conclusion: 基于模糊逻辑的奖励塑形能有效整合专家知识，增强强化学习在复杂任务中的探索能力和鲁棒性，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) often struggles in real-world tasks with high-dimensional state spaces and long horizons, where sparse or fixed rewards severely slow down exploration and cause agents to get trapped in local optima. This paper presents a fuzzy logic based reward shaping method that integrates human intuition into RL reward design. By encoding expert knowledge into adaptive and interpreable terms, fuzzy rules promote stable learning and reduce sensitivity to hyperparameters. The proposed method leverages these properties to adapt reward contributions based on the agent state, enabling smoother transitions between fast motion and precise control in challenging navigation tasks. Extensive simulation results on autonomous drone racing benchmarks show stable learning behavior and consistent task performance across scenarios of increasing difficulty. The proposed method achieves faster convergence and reduced performance variability across training seeds in more challenging environments, with success rates improving by up to approximately 5 percent compared to non fuzzy reward formulations.

</details>


### [123] [From Seeing to Simulating: Generative High-Fidelity Simulation with Digital Cousins for Generalizable Robot Learning and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15805)
*Jasper Lu,Zhenhao Shen,Yuanfei Wang,Shugao Liu,Shengqiang Xu,Shawn Xie,Jingkai Xu,Feng Jiang,Jade Yang,Chen Xie,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种生成式框架，将真实世界全景图映射到高保真仿真场景，并通过语义和几何编辑生成多样化场景，支持机器人操作与导航任务，实验表明该方法能显著提升模型在未见场景中的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境中数据采集成本高，缺乏多样性，限制了机器人策略的鲁棒性学习；需要一种高效、可扩展的数据增强方式来支持通用机器人学习。

Method: 提出生成式real-to-sim框架，从真实全景图像生成高保真仿真场景，结合语义与几何编辑生成‘数字表亲’场景，并通过多房间拼接构建大规模连贯环境，利用高质量物理引擎支持交互任务。

Result: 实验证明仿真与现实具有强相关性，大规模数据生成显著提升了模型在未见场景和物体变化下的泛化性能。

Conclusion: Digital Cousins框架为可泛化的机器人学习与评估提供了一个高效、逼真的数据增强平台，验证了基于生成式场景扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Learning robust robot policies in real-world environments requires diverse data augmentation, yet scaling real-world data collection is costly due to the need for acquiring physical assets and reconfiguring environments. Therefore, augmenting real-world scenes into simulation has become a practical augmentation for efficient learning and evaluation. We present a generative framework that establishes a generative real-to-sim mapping from real-world panoramas to high-fidelity simulation scenes, and further synthesize diverse cousin scenes via semantic and geometric editing. Combined with high-quality physics engines and realistic assets, the generated scenes support interactive manipulation tasks. Additionally, we incorporate multi-room stitching to construct consistent large-scale environments for long-horizon navigation across complex layouts. Experiments demonstrate a strong sim-to-real correlation validating our platform's fidelity, and show that extensively scaling up data generation leads to significantly better generalization to unseen scene and object variations, demonstrating the effectiveness of Digital Cousins for generalizable robot learning and evaluation.

</details>


### [124] [Limits of Lamarckian Evolution Under Pressure of Morphological Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15854)
*Jed R Muff,Karine Miras,A. E. Eiben*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 拉马克进化在机器人形态与控制器协同进化中通过继承父代学习的控制器提升性能，但在强调形态多样性的选择压力下，因亲子形态相似性降低而导致性能显著下降，暴露出继承利用与多样性探索之间的根本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在高形态变异的选择压力下，拉马克遗传相对于达尔文遗传的有效性及其局限性，特别是在形态新颖性被奖励的情况下。

Method: 使用可进化和学习的模块化机器人系统，比较纯任务导向选择与兼顾形态新颖性的多目标选择下，拉马克与达尔文进化的表现差异。

Result: 仅优化任务性能时，拉马克进化优于达尔文进化；但引入形态多样性选择后，拉马克系统的性能大幅下降，且降幅远超达尔文系统，原因是亲子形态相似性降低削弱了控制器继承的优势。

Conclusion: 拉马克进化在追求形态多样性的环境中存在根本限制，揭示了基于继承的开发与多样性驱动的探索之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Lamarckian inheritance has been shown to be a powerful accelerator in systems where the joint evolution of robot morphologies and controllers is enhanced with individual learning. Its defining advantage lies in the offspring inheriting controllers learned by their parents. The efficacy of this option, however, relies on morphological similarity between parent and offspring. In this study, we examine how Lamarckian inheritance performs when the search process is driven toward high morphological variance, potentially straining the requirement for parent-offspring similarity. Using a system of modular robots that can evolve and learn to solve a locomotion task, we compare Darwinian and Lamarckian evolution to determine how they respond to shifting from pure task-based selection to a multi-objective pressure that also rewards morphological novelty. Our results confirm that Lamarckian evolution outperforms Darwinian evolution when optimizing task-performance alone. However, introducing selection pressure for morphological diversity causes a substantial performance drop, which is much greater in the Lamarckian system. Further analyses show that promoting diversity reduces parent-offspring similarity, which in turn reduces the benefits of inheriting controllers learned by parents. These results reveal the limits of Lamarckian evolution by exposing a fundamental trade-off between inheritance-based exploitation and diversity-driven exploration.

</details>


### [125] [Environment-Adaptive Solid-State LiDAR-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15864)
*Zhi Zhang,Chalermchon Satirapod,Bingtao Ma,Changjun Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种环境自适应的固态LiDAR-惯性里程计方法，通过引入局部法向量约束和退化感知的地图维护策略，提升极端环境下的定位精度和建图鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极端环境中，由于几何退化严重和观测不可靠，现有固态LiDAR-inertial SLAM难以实现精确建图，导致优化病态和地图不一致问题。

Method: 引入局部法向量约束以增强状态估计稳定性，并设计退化引导的地图更新策略来提高地图精度，从而在后续估计中进一步提升定位准确性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在极端和感知退化环境中相比基线方法平均RMSE降低了最多12.8%，显著提升了建图精度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法通过融合局部法向量约束与退化感知地图维护，在恶劣环境下实现了更准确、更稳定的LiDAR-inertial状态估计，有效缓解了几何退化带来的负面影响。

Abstract: Solid-state LiDAR-inertial SLAM has attracted significant attention due to its advantages in speed and robustness. However, achieving accurate mapping in extreme environments remains challenging due to severe geometric degeneracy and unreliable observations, which often lead to ill-conditioned optimization and map inconsistencies. To address these challenges, we propose an environment-adaptive solid-state LiDAR-inertial odometry that integrates local normal-vector constraints with degeneracy-aware map maintenance to enhance localization accuracy. Specifically, we introduce local normal-vector constraints to improve the stability of state estimation, effectively suppressing localization drift in degenerate scenarios. Furthermore, we design a degeneration-guided map update strategy to improve map precision. Benefiting from the refined map representation, localization accuracy is further enhanced in subsequent estimation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior mapping accuracy and robustness in extreme and perceptually degraded environments, with an average RMSE reduction of up to 12.8% compared to the baseline method.

</details>


### [126] [DTEA: A Dual-Topology Elastic Actuator Enabling Real-Time Switching Between Series and Parallel Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15865)
*Vishal Ramesh,Aman Singh,Shishir Kolathaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型双拓扑弹性驱动器（DTEA），可在串联弹性（SEA）和并联弹性（PEA）模式间实时切换。原型机实验验证了其切换机制的鲁棒性和快速性，切换时间小于33.33毫秒，且在负载下完成324次循环无损伤。两种模式的刚度和抗扰性能符合传统特性，证明了DTEA的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弹性驱动器无法在运行中实时切换串联（SEA）和并联（PEA）拓扑，限制了其在多任务环境中的适应性。本文旨在设计一种可动态切换拓扑结构的驱动器，以兼具两者优势。

Method: 提出双拓扑弹性驱动器（DTEA）设计，集成可在SEA与PEA之间动态切换的机械结构，并研制原理样机。通过加载下的切换循环实验评估切换机制的鲁棒性和响应时间，同时开展静态刚度和扰动抑制实验，分别表征两种模式的性能。

Result: DTEA在负载下成功完成324次切换循环无损坏，切换时间小于33.33 ms。PEA模式刚度为8.54 ± 0.02 Nm/rad，SEA模式为5.57 ± 0.02 Nm/rad，前者是后者的1.53倍。扰动实验显示，SEA模式峰值偏转为5.2度，是PEA模式（2.3度）的2.26倍；平均调节时间为1380 ms，是PEA模式（400 ms）的3.45倍。

Conclusion: DTEA能够稳定、快速地在SEA和PEA模式间切换，兼具两种传统拓扑的力学特性，验证了其在运行中动态重构拓扑的可行性，为高适应性机器人驱动提供了新方案。

Abstract: Series and parallel elastic actuators offer complementary but mutually exclusive advantages, yet no existing actuator enables real-time transition between these topologies during operation. This paper presents a novel actuator design called the Dual-Topology Elastic Actuator (DTEA), which enables dynamic switching between SEA and PEA topologies during operation. A proof-of-concept prototype of the DTEA is developed to demonstrate the feasibility of the topology-switching mechanism. Experiments are conducted to evaluate the robustness and timing of the switching mechanism under operational conditions. The actuator successfully performed 324 topology-switching cycles under load without damage, demonstrating the robustness of the mechanism. The measured switching time between SEA and PEA modes is under 33.33 ms. Additional experiments are conducted to characterize the static stiffness and disturbance rejection performance in both SEA and PEA modes. Static stiffness tests show that the PEA mode is 1.53x stiffer than the SEA mode, with KSEA = 5.57 +/- 0.02 Nm/rad and KPEA = 8.54 +/- 0.02 Nm/rad. Disturbance rejection experiments show that the mean peak deflection in SEA mode is 2.26x larger than in PEA mode (5.2 deg vs. 2.3 deg), while the mean settling time is 3.45x longer (1380 ms vs. 400 ms). The observed behaviors are consistent with the known characteristics of conventional SEA and PEA actuators, validating the functionality of both modes in the DTEA actuator.

</details>


### [127] [Robust Fleet Sizing for Multi-UAV Inspection Missions under Synchronized Replacement Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15890)
*Vishal Ramesh,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多无人机巡检任务的闭式解队列规模规则k = m(ceil(R) + 1)，通过增加m个备用无人机来吸收最坏情况下的同步充电需求，确保任务级可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法假设稳态运行条件或将更换请求视为独立事件，无法应对有限时间任务中因电池耗尽同步导致的备用无人机集中需求问题。

Method: 推导出一种闭式解的队列规模规则，并使用蒙特卡洛模拟在五种不同场景下进行验证，考虑了恢复与活动时间比、风速变化等因素。

Result: 与基于Erlang-B模型的方法相比，新规则在所有测试条件下均保持99.8%的任务成功率（95%置信下限为99.3%），而Erlang-B方法在R=3.39时成功率降至69.9%，且87%的备用耗尽事件集中在最高十分位的5分钟需求窗口内。

Conclusion: 所提出的队列规模规则能有效应对多无人机系统中电池耗尽同步带来的突发性替换需求，显著提升任务可靠性，同时仅需少量额外备用无人机。

Abstract: Multi-UAV inspection missions require spare drones to replace active drones during recharging cycles. Existing fleet-sizing approaches often assume steady-state operating conditions that do not apply to finite-horizon missions, or they treat replacement requests as statistically independent events. The latter provides per-request blocking guarantees that fail to translate to mission-level reliability when demands cluster. This paper identifies a structural failure mode where efficient routing assigns similar workloads to each UAV, leading to synchronized battery depletion and replacement bursts that exhaust the spare pool even when average capacity is sufficient.
  We derive a closed-form sufficient fleet-sizing rule, k = m(ceil(R) + 1), where m is the number of active UAVs and R is the recovery-to-active time ratio. This additive buffer of m spares absorbs worst-case synchronized demand at recovery-cycle boundaries and ensures mission-level reliability even when all UAVs deplete simultaneously. Monte Carlo validation across five scenarios (m in [2, 10], R in [0.87, 3.39], 1000 trials each) shows that Erlang-B sizing with a per-request blocking target epsilon = 0.01 drops to 69.9% mission success at R = 3.39, with 95% of spare exhaustion events concentrated in the top-decile 5-minute demand windows. In contrast, the proposed rule maintains 99.8% success (Wilson 95% lower bound 99.3%) across all tested conditions, including wind variability up to CV = 0.30, while requiring only four additional drones in the most demanding scenario.

</details>


### [128] [A Reconfigurable Pneumatic Joint Enabling Localized Selective Stiffening and Shape Locking in Vine-Inspired Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15907)
*Ayodele James Oyejide,Ustaz A. Yaqub,Samir Erturk,Eray A. Baran,Fabio Stroppa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可重构气动关节（RPJ）结构，用于增强软体生长机器人的局部刚度，同时保持其连续生长能力，从而提升其在自由空间中的形状保持、负载能力和操控性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的藤蔓式生长机器人在自由空间中应用受限，主要由于轴向刚度低、承载能力差以及转向后无法保持形状。本文旨在通过引入可调刚度机制来克服这些限制。

Method: 设计并集成对称分布的气动腔室作为可重构气动关节（RPJ），通过加压实现局部弯曲刚度增加；结合腱驱动转向系统和中空蒸发基座，实现连续生长与局部刚度调控的解耦。

Result: 实验表明该机器人在适度压力下即可实现有效蒸发与局部刚化，刚化性能与层间阻塞机制相当；具备良好的弯曲形状保持能力、负载下抗重力变形能力、级联回缩功能，并能在自由空间中稳定运输高达202克的有效载荷。

Conclusion: RPJ机制为实现结构自适应的藤蔓机器人提供了可行路径，有望应用于无约束环境中的操作任务，如物体分拣与自适应探索。

Abstract: Vine-inspired robots achieve large workspace coverage through tip eversion, enabling safe navigation in confined and cluttered environments. However, their deployment in free space is fundamentally limited by low axial stiffness, poor load-bearing capacity, and the inability to retain shape during and after steering. In this work, we propose a reconfigurable pneumatic joint (RPJ) architecture that introduces discrete, pressure-tunable stiffness along the robot body without compromising continuous growth. Each RPJ module comprises symmetrically distributed pneumatic chambers that locally increase bending stiffness when pressurized, enabling decoupling between global compliance and localized rigidity. We integrate the RPJs into a soft growing robot with tendon-driven steering and develop a compact base station for mid-air eversion. System characterization and experimental validation demonstrate moderate pressure requirements for eversion, as well as comparable localized stiffening and steering performance to layer-jamming mechanisms. Demonstrations further show that the proposed robot achieves improved shape retention during bending, reduced gravitational deflection under load, cascading retraction, and reliable payload transport up to 202 g in free space. The RPJ mechanism establishes a practical pathway toward structurally adaptive vine robots for manipulation-oriented tasks such as object sorting and adaptive exploration in unconstrained environments.

</details>


### [129] [DENALI: A Dataset Enabling Non-Line-of-Sight Spatial Reasoning with Low-Cost LiDARs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16201)
*Nikhil Behari,Diego Rivero,Luke Apostolides,Suman Ghosh,Paul Pu Liang,Ramesh Raskar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了DENALI，首个大规模真实世界低代价LiDAR时空直方图数据集，用于实现隐藏物体的非视距（NLOS）感知，展示了通过数据驱动方法在消费级LiDAR上实现高精度NLOS感知的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级LiDAR虽然硬件受限，但内部记录的时间分辨直方图包含多路径反射光信息，蕴含丰富的非视距（NLOS）线索，传统方法难以有效利用，因此需要探索数据驱动的方法来挖掘其潜力。

Method: 构建了DENALI数据集，包含72,000个隐藏物体场景下的时间分辨LiDAR直方图，覆盖多样化的物体形状、位置、光照条件和空间分辨率，并基于该数据集训练和评估数据驱动模型以实现NLOS感知。

Result: 实验证明消费级LiDAR可通过数据驱动方法实现准确的NLOS感知，同时识别出影响性能的关键场景与建模因素以及仿真到现实的保真度差距。

Conclusion: DENALI为基于低成本LiDAR的可扩展NLOS视觉研究提供了基础，推动未来在真实场景中实现高效非视距感知的发展。

Abstract: Consumer LiDARs in mobile devices and robots typically output a single depth value per pixel. Yet internally, they record full time-resolved histograms containing direct and multi-bounce light returns; these multi-bounce returns encode rich non-line-of-sight (NLOS) cues that can enable perception of hidden objects in a scene. However, severe hardware limitations of consumer LiDARs make NLOS reconstruction with conventional methods difficult. In this work, we motivate a complementary direction: enabling NLOS perception with low-cost LiDARs through data-driven inference. We present DENALI, the first large-scale real-world dataset of space-time histograms from low-cost LiDARs capturing hidden objects. We capture time-resolved LiDAR histograms for 72,000 hidden-object scenes across diverse object shapes, positions, lighting conditions, and spatial resolutions. Using our dataset, we show that consumer LiDARs can enable accurate, data-driven NLOS perception. We further identify key scene and modeling factors that limit performance, as well as simulation-fidelity gaps that hinder current sim-to-real transfer, motivating future work toward scalable NLOS vision with consumer LiDARs.

</details>


### [130] [Semantic Area Graph Reasoning for Multi-Robot Language-Guided Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16263)
*Ruiyang Wang,Hao-Lun Hsu,Jiwoo Kim,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为语义区域图推理（SAGR）的分层框架，利用大语言模型协调多机器人进行语义搜索，通过结构化的语义-拓扑环境表征提升在复杂室内环境中的目标搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多机器人协调策略无法有效融入高层任务意图（如按房间类型搜索特定物体），难以支持需要语义推理的探索任务。

Method: 构建一个语义区域图，从语义占据地图中逐步提取房间实例、连通性、前沿信息和机器人状态，并将其作为大语言模型推理的紧凑任务相关表示；由大语言模型进行基于空间结构和任务上下文的高层语义房间分配，底层则采用确定性前沿规划与局部导航执行几何探索。

Result: 在Habitat-Matterport3D数据集的100个场景中实验表明，SAGR在保持与最先进探索方法相当的探索效率的同时，显著提升了语义目标搜索效率，在大型环境中最高提升达18.8%。

Conclusion: 结构化的语义抽象可作为大语言模型推理与多机器人协调之间的有效接口，有助于在复杂室内环境中实现更智能的语义驱动探索。

Abstract: Coordinating multi-robot systems (MRS) to search in unknown environments is particularly challenging for tasks that require semantic reasoning beyond geometric exploration. Classical coordination strategies rely on frontier coverage or information gain and cannot incorporate high-level task intent, such as searching for objects associated with specific room types. We propose \textit{Semantic Area Graph Reasoning} (SAGR), a hierarchical framework that enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to coordinate multi-robot exploration and semantic search through a structured semantic-topological abstraction of the environment. SAGR incrementally constructs a semantic area graph from a semantic occupancy map, encoding room instances, connectivity, frontier availability, and robot states into a compact task-relevant representation for LLM reasoning. The LLM performs high-level semantic room assignment based on spatial structure and task context, while deterministic frontier planning and local navigation handle geometric execution within assigned rooms. Experiments on the Habitat-Matterport3D dataset across 100 scenarios show that SAGR remains competitive with state-of-the-art exploration methods while consistently improving semantic target search efficiency, with up to 18.8\% in large environments. These results highlight the value of structured semantic abstractions as an effective interface between LLM-based reasoning and multi-robot coordination in complex indoor environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [GIST: Multimodal Knowledge Extraction and Spatial Grounding via Intelligent Semantic Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15495)
*Shivendra Agrawal,Bradley Hayes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GIST是一个多模态知识提取管道，将消费级移动点云转化为语义标注的导航拓扑，支持在复杂环境中进行智能人机交互任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集且语义分布长尾的动态环境中，传统视觉方法和现有VLM难以实现良好的空间定位与语义理解，需要更鲁棒的语义拓扑结构来提升导航性能。

Method: 提出GIST框架：将点云转换为2D占据栅格图，提取拓扑结构，并通过智能关键帧和语义选择叠加轻量级语义层，支持多种下游任务。

Result: 实现了四项下游任务：语义搜索、单样本语义定位（1.04米top-5平均平移误差）、区域分类和视觉接地指令生成；LLM评估优于基线，在N=5实地测试中达到80%仅靠语音提示的导航成功率。

Conclusion: GIST能有效构建语义丰富的导航拓扑，显著提升复杂环境中的空间接地能力，具备通用设计潜力和实际部署价值。

Abstract: Navigating complex, densely packed environments like retail stores, warehouses, and hospitals poses a significant spatial grounding challenge for humans and embodied AI. In these spaces, dense visual features quickly become stale given the quasi-static nature of items, and long-tail semantic distributions challenge traditional computer vision. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) help assistive systems navigate semantically-rich spaces, they still struggle with spatial grounding in cluttered environments. We present GIST (Grounded Intelligent Semantic Topology), a multimodal knowledge extraction pipeline that transforms a consumer-grade mobile point cloud into a semantically annotated navigation topology. Our architecture distills the scene into a 2D occupancy map, extracts its topological layout, and overlays a lightweight semantic layer via intelligent keyframe and semantic selection. We demonstrate the versatility of this structured spatial knowledge through critical downstream Human-AI interaction tasks: (1) an intent-driven Semantic Search engine that actively infers categorical alternatives and zones when exact matches fail; (2) a one-shot Semantic Localizer achieving a 1.04 m top-5 mean translation error; (3) a Zone Classification module that segments the walkable floor plan into high-level semantic regions; and (4) a Visually-Grounded Instruction Generator that synthesizes optimal paths into egocentric, landmark-rich natural language routing. In multi-criteria LLM evaluations, GIST outperforms sequence-based instruction generation baselines. Finally, an in-situ formative evaluation (N=5) yields an 80% navigation success rate relying solely on verbal cues, validating the system's capacity for universal design.

</details>


### [132] [LACE: Lattice Attention for Cross-thread Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15529)
*Yang Li,Zirui Zhang,Yang Liu,Chengzhi Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LACE框架通过引入跨线程注意力机制，使大语言模型的并行推理路径能够交互和纠错，显著提升了推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在推理时各路径相互独立，容易以相同方式失败，缺乏协同与信息共享。

Method: 提出LACE框架，利用模型架构改造实现跨线程注意力，并构建合成数据管道训练模型进行线程间通信与错误纠正。

Result: 实验显示LACE比标准并行搜索推理准确率提升超过7个百分点。

Conclusion: 允许并行推理路径交互可显著增强大语言模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Current large language models reason in isolation. Although it is common to sample multiple reasoning paths in parallel, these trajectories do not interact, and often fail in the same redundant ways. We introduce LACE, a framework that transforms reasoning from a collection of independent trials into a coordinated, parallel process. By repurposing the model architecture to enable cross-thread attention, LACE allows concurrent reasoning paths to share intermediate insights and correct one another during inference. A central challenge is the absence of natural training data that exhibits such collaborative behavior. We address this gap with a synthetic data pipeline that explicitly teaches models to communicate and error-correct across threads. Experiments show that this unified exploration substantially outperforms standard parallel search, improving reasoning accuracy by over 7 points. Our results suggest that large language models can be more effective when parallel reasoning paths are allowed to interact.

</details>


### [133] [Preregistered Belief Revision Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15558)
*Saad Alqithami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PBRC（预注册信念修正契约）的协议机制，用于解决多智能体系统中因社会互动导致的错误共识问题。该机制通过将通信与可信的知识更新分离，确保所有信念变化基于可验证的外部证据，并具备可审计性和可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协商式多智能体系统中，智能体之间的交流可能导致将多数意见或权威视为证据的“从众效应”，从而高置信度地收敛到错误结论。为防止此类认知风险，需要一种机制来区分社会影响与真正的证据驱动更新。

Method: 提出PBRC协议，要求在非回退步骤中必须引用预注册的触发条件并提供经过外部验证的证据令牌集合。定义了证据触发、允许的修正算子、优先级规则和回退策略，并引入契约动态信念逻辑以形式化轨迹不变量。

Result: 证明在保守回退的证据契约下，仅社会交互轮次无法提升置信度或引发纯从众型错误共识；展示了可审计协议存在保持信念轨迹的标准形式；实现了认知问责，即任何顶层假设变更均可追溯至具体验证证据；对于令牌不变契约，证明轨迹仅依赖于令牌暴露序列，并在泛洪传播下由截断可达性精确刻画，给出普遍证据闭合的紧致直径界。

Conclusion: PBRC机制有效抑制了多智能体系统中的错误共识级联，提供了信念更新的可审计性与问责性，平衡了鲁棒性与活性之间的权衡，为安全关键型协作推理系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Deliberative multi-agent systems allow agents to exchange messages and revise beliefs over time. While this interaction is meant to improve performance, it can also create dangerous conformity effects: agreement, confidence, prestige, or majority size may be treated as if they were evidence, producing high-confidence convergence to false conclusions. To address this, we introduce PBRC (Preregistered Belief Revision Contracts), a protocol-level mechanism that strictly separates open communication from admissible epistemic change. A PBRC contract publicly fixes first-order evidence triggers, admissible revision operators, a priority rule, and a fallback policy. A non-fallback step is accepted only when it cites a preregistered trigger and provides a nonempty witness set of externally validated evidence tokens. This ensures that every substantive belief change is both enforceable by a router and auditable after the fact. In this paper, (a) we prove that under evidential contracts with conservative fallback, social-only rounds cannot increase confidence and cannot generate purely conformity-driven wrong-but-sure cascades. (b) We show that auditable trigger protocols admit evidential PBRC normal forms that preserve belief trajectories and canonicalized audit traces. (c) We demonstrate that sound enforcement yields epistemic accountability: any change of top hypothesis is attributable to a concrete validated witness set. For token-invariant contracts, (d) we prove that enforced trajectories depend only on token-exposure traces; under flooding dissemination, these traces are characterized exactly by truncated reachability, giving tight diameter bounds for universal evidence closure. Finally, we introduce a companion contractual dynamic doxastic logic to specify trace invariants, and provide simulations illustrating cascade suppression, auditability, and robustness-liveness trade-offs.

</details>


### [134] [LLM Reasoning Is Latent, Not the Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15726)
*Wenshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为大语言模型的推理应被视为潜在状态轨迹的形成，而非表面的思维链（CoT），并提出三个假设，当前证据最支持以潜在状态轨迹为主要研究对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分LLM推理中常被混淆的因素，澄清推理的本质对象，以改进可解释性、基准测试和干预方法。

Method: 提出三种竞争性假设（H1: 潜在状态轨迹；H2: 表面CoT；H0: 通用序列计算），整合实证与机制研究，并通过计算审计示例分解表面痕迹、潜在干预和计算预算。

Result: 现有证据最强烈支持H1，即推理主要由潜在状态轨迹介导，但并非适用于所有任务的最终结论。

Conclusion: 应将潜在状态动态作为LLM推理的默认研究对象，并设计能明确分离表面痕迹、潜在状态和序列计算的推理评估方法。

Abstract: This position paper argues that large language model (LLM) reasoning should be studied as latent-state trajectory formation rather than as faithful surface chain-of-thought (CoT). This matters because claims about faithfulness, interpretability, reasoning benchmarks, and inference-time intervention all depend on what the field takes the primary object of reasoning to be. We ask what that object should be once three often-confounded factors are separated and formalize three competing hypotheses: H1, reasoning is primarily mediated by latent-state trajectories; H2, reasoning is primarily mediated by explicit surface CoT; and H0, most apparent reasoning gains are better explained by generic serial compute than by any privileged representational object. Reorganizing recent empirical, mechanistic, and survey work under this framework, and adding compute-audited worked exemplars that factorize surface traces, latent interventions, and matched budget expansions, we find that current evidence most strongly supports H1 as a default working hypothesis rather than as a task-independent verdict. We therefore make two recommendations: the field should treat latent-state dynamics as the default object of study for LLM reasoning, and it should evaluate reasoning with designs that explicitly disentangle surface traces, latent states, and serial compute.

</details>


### [135] [Structured Abductive-Deductive-Inductive Reasoning for LLMs via Algebraic Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15727)
*Sankalp Gilda,Shlok Gilda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于皮尔士三重推理（溯因、演绎、归纳）的符号推理框架，通过五个代数不变量（尤其是 weakest link 约束）确保大语言模型推理链中的逻辑一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在结构化逻辑推理中存在系统性缺陷，如混淆假设生成与验证、无法区分推测与已验证知识、弱推理步骤传播等，亟需一种能保证逻辑一致性的推理机制。

Method: 设计了一个符号推理框架，形式化实现皮尔士的溯因、演绎和归纳推理，并引入五个代数不变量（Gamma Quintet），其中 weakest link bound 约束确保推理链中任何结论的可靠性不超过其最弱前提。通过基于属性的测试套件（100个性质和16个模糊测试，超过10^5个生成案例）验证所有不变量。

Result: 实现了对五个逻辑不变量的有效验证，提供了可验证的参考实现，能够防止多步推理中逻辑不一致的积累，在链式思维推理中得到经验验证。

Conclusion: 该框架为大语言模型辅助推理提供了一个逻辑一致、可验证的基础，可作为未来推理基准的可靠基石。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit systematic limitations in structured logical reasoning: they conflate hypothesis generation with verification, cannot distinguish conjecture from validated knowledge, and allow weak reasoning steps to propagate unchecked through inference chains. We present a symbolic reasoning scaffold that operationalizes Peirce's tripartite inference -- abduction, deduction, and induction -- as an explicit protocol for LLM-assisted reasoning. The framework enforces logical consistency through five algebraic invariants (the Gamma Quintet), the strongest of which -- the Weakest Link bound -- ensures that no conclusion in a reasoning chain can exceed the reliability of its least-supported premise. This principle, independently grounded as weakest link resolution in possibilistic logic and empirically validated for chain-of-thought reasoning, prevents logical inconsistencies from accumulating across multi-step inference. We verify all invariants through a property-based testing suite of 100 properties and 16 fuzz tests over 10^5+ generated cases, providing a verified reference implementation of the invariants suitable as a foundation for future reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [136] [KWBench: Measuring Unprompted Problem Recognition in Knowledge Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15760)
*Ankit Maloo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KWBench是一个新的基准，用于评估大语言模型在无提示情况下识别专业场景问题的能力，强调问题识别而非任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多集中于任务完成或信息提取，而忽略了模型在没有明确提示时识别问题本质的能力。KWBench旨在填补这一空白，评估模型在真实知识工作中自主识别问题结构的能力。

Method: 构建包含223个来自实践者的任务的基准，每个任务基于博弈论模式并附有专家标注的实际情况和预期失败模式；模型仅接收原始数据和任务提示，评分采用三层次标准，包含强制性联合检查。

Result: 评估了16个模型，最佳模型通过率为27.9%，前两名模型仅在31.7%的任务上达成一致；前8名模型中，44项任务仅由单一模型解决，组合覆盖率达50.7%；通过任务后质量得分趋同（约83%），但整体未通过率仍高。

Conclusion: 当前大模型在无提示下识别复杂专业问题的能力仍然有限，不同模型表现差异大，需结合多个模型以提升覆盖能力；KWBench的发布旨在推动对模型问题识别能力的重视，超越传统的任务执行评估范式。

Abstract: We introduce the first version of KWBench (Knowledge Work Bench), a benchmark for unprompted problem recognition in large language models: can an LLM identify a professional scenario before attempting to solve it. Existing frontier benchmarks have saturated, and most knowledge-work evaluations to date reduce to extraction or task completion against a specification. KWBench targets the step before that: recognizing the governing structure of the situation from raw inputs alone.
  The benchmark contains 223 tasks sourced from practitioners across acquisitions, contract negotiations, clinical pharmacy, organizational politics, fraud analysis, and incentive design. Each task encodes a formal game-theoretic pattern (principal-agent conflict, signaling, mechanism design failure, strategic omission, coalitional dynamics, strategic interdependence) and carries structured ground truth recording the expert reading of the situation and the anticipated failure modes. Models receive raw data and a task prompt with no indication of problem type. Scoring is a three-tier rubric gated by a mandatory conjunctive check. Mandatory criteria encode the predicted wrong paths.
  We evaluate 16 models. The best model passes on 27.9% of tasks. The top two models agree on only 31.7% of their passes. Among the top 8, 44 tasks are solved by exactly one model; routing across the top 8 covers 50.7% of the benchmark, nearly double the best single model. Conditional on passing, quality scores converge (approx 83% across models); unconditional scores do not. Same models articulate the relevant game-theoretic concept correctly when asked, then fail to apply it unprompted. We release KWBench to shift how frontier models are evaluated on knowledge work, scoring them on whether they recognize the right problem from the situation alone, not only on how well they execute once the problem has been framed for them.

</details>


### [137] [Stein Variational Black-Box Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15837)
*Thomas Landais,Olivier Goudet,Adrien Goëffon,Frédéric Saubion,Sylvain Lamprier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Stein算子的分布估计算法，通过在参数空间中引入粒子间的排斥机制，增强群体在高维组合黑箱优化中的探索能力，避免早熟收敛，并在多个基准问题上表现出与现有最先进方法相当甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维组合黑箱优化中，传统分布估计算法（EDAs）容易过早收敛于单一区域，难以有效探索多峰目标函数的多个最优解，因此需要一种能平衡探索与开发的新方法。

Method: 引入Stein算子，在分布估计算法中构建粒子间的排斥机制，促使种群在参数空间中分散，联合探索适应度景观的多个模式，从而提升全局搜索能力。

Result: 在多种基准问题上的实验表明，该方法在性能上与当前最先进的方法相当，且在多个大规模实例上表现更优。

Conclusion: Stein变分梯度下降为解决大规模、计算昂贵的离散黑箱优化问题提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Combinatorial black-box optimization in high-dimensional settings demands a careful trade-off between exploiting promising regions of the search space and preserving sufficient exploration to identify multiple optima. Although Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms (EDAs) provide a powerful model-based framework, they often concentrate on a single region of interest, which may result in premature convergence when facing complex or multimodal objective landscapes. In this work, we incorporate the Stein operator to introduce a repulsive mechanism among particles in the parameter space, thereby encouraging the population to disperse and jointly explore several modes of the fitness landscape. Empirical evaluations across diverse benchmark problems show that the proposed method achieves performance competitive with, and in several cases superior to, leading state-of-the-art approaches, particularly on large-scale instances. These findings highlight the potential of Stein variational gradient descent as a promising direction for addressing large, computationally expensive, discrete black-box optimization problems.

</details>


### [138] [Discover and Prove: An Open-source Agentic Framework for Hard Mode Automated Theorem Proving in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15839)
*Chengwu Liu,Yichun Yin,Ye Yuan,Jiaxuan Xie,Botao Li,Siqi Li,Jianhao Shen,Yan Xu,Lifeng Shang,Ming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“Hard Mode”这一更严格的ATP（自动定理证明）评估模式，以替代传统依赖嵌入答案的“Easy Mode”，并发布了MiniF2F-Hard和FIMO-Hard两个新基准。同时提出Discover And Prove (DAP) 框架，利用大语言模型的自然语言推理与自我反思能力先发现答案，再将问题转换为Easy Mode进行形式化证明，显著提升了在Hard Mode下的定理证明性能，揭示了当前大模型在答案准确率上远超形式化证明系统的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ATP基准通常将答案直接嵌入问题中（Easy Mode），这简化了任务，无法真实反映模型在无人类辅助下独立发现答案并证明的能力，导致对模型能力的高估。因此需要一种更贴近人类解题过程、更具挑战性的评估方式（Hard Mode）来准确衡量系统的真实水平。

Method: 首先构建了两个Hard Mode的基准数据集MiniF2F-Hard和FIMO-Hard；然后提出Discover And Prove (DAP) 框架：利用大语言模型通过自然语言推理和显式自我反思来独立发现答案，随后将Hard Mode的问题重写为Easy Mode格式，交由现有的ATP证明器完成形式化证明。

Result: DAP在CombiBench上将解决的问题数从之前的16次采样最多7个提升至10个；在PutnamBench上首次实现了36个定理的Hard Mode形式化证明。同时发现当前最先进的大语言模型在这些问题上的答案准确率超过80%，而形式化证明系统不足10%，揭示了两者之间的巨大差距。

Conclusion: Hard Mode是一种更能反映真实推理难度的评估标准，DAP框架有效结合了大语言模型的推理能力和现有ATP系统的证明能力，在Hard Mode下取得当前最优表现，同时凸显了仅依赖嵌入答案的Easy Mode可能掩盖模型在独立发现答案方面的能力缺陷。

Abstract: Most ATP benchmarks embed the final answer within the formal statement -- a convention we call "Easy Mode" -- a design that simplifies the task relative to what human competitors face and may lead to optimistic estimates of model capability. We call the stricter, more realistic setting "Hard Mode": the system must independently discover the answer before constructing a formal proof. To enable Hard Mode research, we make two contributions. First, we release MiniF2F-Hard and FIMO-Hard, expert-reannotated Hard Mode variants of two widely-used ATP benchmarks. Second, we introduce Discover And Prove (DAP), an agentic framework that uses LLM natural-language reasoning with explicit self-reflection to discover answers, then rewrites Hard Mode statements into Easy Mode ones for existing ATP provers. DAP sets the state of the art: on CombiBench it raises solved problems from 7 (previous SOTA, Pass@16) to 10; on PutnamBench it is the first system to formally prove 36 theorems in Hard Mode -- while simultaneously revealing that state-of-the-art LLMs exceed 80% answer accuracy on the same problems where formal provers manage under 10%, exposing a substantial gap that Hard Mode benchmarks are uniquely suited to measure.

</details>


### [139] [Towards Rigorous Explainability by Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15898)
*Olivier Létoffé,Xuanxiang Huang,Joao Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文概述了在可解释人工智能（XAI）中使用严格符号方法替代非严谨非符号方法的持续努力，特别是在特征重要性分配方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非符号方法缺乏严谨性，可能误导人类决策者，尤其是在高风险的机器学习应用中。

Method: 综述现有文献，强调采用严格的符号化XAI方法来解决非符号方法（如SHAP中的Shapley值）缺乏可证明严谨性的问题。

Result: 提出符号方法作为更可靠、可验证的替代方案，用于特征重要性解释。

Conclusion: 为了提高可解释性与可信度，应推动从非符号、非严谨方法转向基于形式化逻辑的符号化XAI方法。

Abstract: For around a decade, non-symbolic methods have been the option of choice when explaining complex machine learning (ML) models. Unfortunately, such methods lack rigor and can mislead human decision-makers. In high-stakes uses of ML, the lack of rigor is especially problematic. One prime example of provable lack of rigor is the adoption of Shapley values in explainable artificial intelligence (XAI), with the tool SHAP being a ubiquitous example. This paper overviews the ongoing efforts towards using rigorous symbolic methods of XAI as an alternative to non-rigorous non-symbolic approaches, concretely for assigning relative feature importance.

</details>


### [140] [Weak-Link Optimization for Multi-Agent Reasoning and Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15972)
*Haoyu Bian,Chaoning Zhang,Jiaquan Zhang,Xingyao Li,Yuanfang Guo,Wei Dong,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WORC框架，基于弱链原则优化多智能体推理系统中的薄弱环节。通过元学习和群体智能算法定位弱智能体，并采用不确定性驱动的资源分配策略增强其可靠性，从而提升整体系统的准确率、稳定性和跨架构泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的多智能体框架在协作中存在推理不稳定性问题，错误易被放大。当前研究多集中于提升强智能体能力或抑制不可靠输出，缺乏对性能瓶颈（弱智能体）的系统性识别与优化。

Method: WORC框架分为两个阶段：1）弱智能体定位阶段，利用任务特征和基于群体智能算法优化的元学习权重预测器，实现从任务特征到智能体性能权重的零样本映射，识别最低权重者为弱智能体；2）弱链优化阶段，采用不确定性驱动的分配策略，根据预测权重为弱智能体分配更多推理资源（如重复采样次数），以弥补其可靠性不足。

Result: 实验结果显示，WORC在推理基准上平均准确率达到82.2%，显著提升了框架的稳定性与跨架构泛化能力。

Conclusion: 补偿多智能体系统中的弱链比单纯强化强链更能提升系统整体鲁棒性，WORC为构建稳定的多智能体协同推理系统提供了有效路径。

Abstract: LLM-driven multi-agent frameworks address complex reasoning tasks through multi-role collaboration. However, existing approaches often suffer from reasoning instability, where individual agent errors are amplified through collaboration, undermining overall performance. Current research mainly focuses on enhancing high-capability agents or suppressing unreliable outputs to improve framework effectiveness, while systematic identification and reinforcement of performance-limiting agents receive less attention. To address this gap, we propose WORC, a \underline{w}eak-link \underline{o}ptimization framework for multi-agent \underline{r}easoning and \underline{c}ollaboration, grounded in the weak-link principle. WORC follows a two-stage workflow. In the weak agent localization stage, task features are constructed, and a meta-learning-based weight predictor trained on optimal configurations identified by swarm intelligence algorithms (SIAs) enables zero-shot mapping from these features to agent performance weights, where the agent with the lowest predicted weight is identified as the weak agent. In the weak-link optimization stage, an uncertainty-driven allocation strategy assigns additional reasoning budgets to weak agents, with lower predicted weights leading to larger repeated-sampling quotas to compensate for reliability deficiencies. Experimental results show that WORC achieves an average accuracy of 82.2\% on reasoning benchmarks while improving framework stability and cross-architecture generalization, suggesting that compensating for weak links, rather than reinforcing strengths alone, enhances the robustness of multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [141] [ReactBench: A Benchmark for Topological Reasoning in MLLMs on Chemical Reaction Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15994)
*Qiang Xu,Shengyuan Bai,Yu Wang,He Cao,Leqing Chen,Yuanyuan Liu,Bin Feng,Zijing Liu,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReactBench，一个用于评估多模态大语言模型在化学反应图等复杂拓扑结构中结构推理能力的新基准，揭示了现有模型在整体结构理解上的显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要关注语义理解，缺乏对多模态大语言模型在复杂拓扑结构（如分支、循环依赖）中的结构推理能力的评估，因此需要一个新的基准来揭示这一短板。

Method: 构建了一个包含1,618个专家标注问答对的基准ReactBench，基于真实世界的化学反应图，涵盖从线性链到环状图等多种结构，并从四个层次任务维度进行评估。对17种MLLM进行了广泛测试，并通过控制变量实验验证推理瓶颈的存在。

Result: 实验结果显示，在基于锚点的任务与整体结构推理任务之间，模型性能差距超过30%，且消融实验证明该瓶颈源于推理过程而非感知能力。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型在结构推理方面存在根本性缺陷，未来的研究需加强模型对复杂拓扑结构的整体理解能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at recognizing individual visual elements and reasoning over simple linear diagrams. However, when faced with complex topological structures involving branching paths, converging flows, and cyclic dependencies, their reasoning capabilities degrade sharply, even on tasks as basic as counting endpoints. Existing benchmarks fail to probe this gap, focusing on semantic comprehension rather than structural reasoning. We introduce ReactBench, a benchmark that reveals fundamental limitations in structural reasoning through chemical reaction diagrams. These real-world scientific diagrams offer an ideal testbed because they naturally span diverse structures from linear chains to cyclic graphs, while requiring both precise local recognition and coherent global reasoning. Our benchmark comprises 1,618 expert-annotated QA pairs across four hierarchical task dimensions. Extensive evaluation across 17 MLLMs reveals a significant performance gap exceeding 30% between anchor-based tasks and holistic structural reasoning tasks. Controlled ablations confirm this bottleneck lies in reasoning, not perception. These findings expose a fundamental deficit in structural understanding and establish directions for advancing visual reasoning.

</details>


### [142] [MEDLEY-BENCH: Scale Buys Evaluation but Not Control in AI Metacognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16009)
*Farhad Abtahi,Abdolamir Karbalaie,Eduardo Illueca-Fernandez,Fernando Seoane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MEDLEY-BENCH是一个评估AI模型元认知能力的新基准，揭示了模型在自我反思、社会影响和信念修正方面的表现差异，发现评估能力与控制能力脱节，且小模型常可匹敌大模型，表明元认知能力不单纯依赖规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评测缺乏对元认知（如自我监控与调节）的系统评估，尤其在面对模糊任务时的推理监控与修正机制尚不明确。

Method: 提出MEDLEY-BENCH基准，包含独立推理、私有自我修正和受社会影响的修正三个阶段，评估35个模型在五个领域130个模糊实例上的表现，使用MMS和MAS两个评分体系，并进行对抗性分析与相对能力画像。

Result: 发现评估能力随模型规模提升而增强，但控制能力未同步提升；存在两种行为模式：基于论点质量调整与追踪共识统计；所有模型中评估能力均为最弱项，显示普遍存在的‘知行差距’；小型模型常表现优于或媲美大型模型。

Conclusion: 元认知能力不能仅靠模型规模提升获得，未来训练应奖励适度、校准的信念更新，而不仅仅是输出质量，MEDLEY-BENCH为评估社会压力下的信念修正提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Metacognition, the ability to monitor and regulate one's own reasoning, remains under-evaluated in AI benchmarking. We introduce MEDLEY-BENCH, a benchmark of behavioural metacognition that separates independent reasoning, private self-revision, and socially influenced revision under genuine inter-model disagreement. The benchmark evaluates 35 models from 12 families on 130 ambiguous instances across five domains and reports two complementary scores: the Medley Metacognition Score (MMS), a tier-based aggregate of reflective updating, social robustness, and epistemic articulation, and the Medley Ability Score (MAS), derived from four metacognitive sub-abilities. Results show a robust evaluation/control dissociation: evaluation ability increases with model size within families, whereas control does not. In a follow-up progressive adversarial analysis of 11 models, we observed two behavioural profiles, i.e., models that revise primarily in response to argument quality and models that track consensus statistics. Under within-model relative profiling (ipsative scoring), evaluation was the weakest relative ability in all 35 models, indicating a systematic knowing/doing gap. Smaller and cheaper models often matched or outperformed larger counterparts, suggesting that metacognitive competence is not simply a function of scale. These findings position MEDLEY-BENCH as a tool for measuring belief revision under social pressure and suggest that future training should reward calibrated, proportional updating rather than output quality alone.

</details>


### [143] [SocialGrid: A Benchmark for Planning and Social Reasoning in Embodied Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16022)
*Hikaru Shindo,Hanzhao Lin,Lukas Helff,Patrick Schramowski,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SocialGrid是一个受《Among Us》启发的具身多智能体环境，用于评估大语言模型在规划、任务执行和社交推理方面的能力，发现当前模型在社交推理上表现不佳，即使有规划辅助仍难以检测欺骗行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型从文本处理向自主智能体发展，亟需在具身多智能体环境中评估其社交推理能力。

Method: 构建名为SocialGrid的具身多智能体环境，引入可选的规划神谕以分离规划缺陷与社交推理能力，并通过自动失败分析和细粒度指标进行评估，同时建立基于对抗性联赛的Elo排名榜。

Result: 最强开源模型GPT-OSS-120B的任务完成和规划准确率低于60%，常陷入重复行为或无法克服基本障碍；使用规划神谕后任务完成率提升，但社交推理仍是瓶颈，模型检测欺骗的表现接近随机水平，依赖浅层启发式策略而非积累行为证据。

Conclusion: SocialGrid为评估和改进LLM智能体的社交推理提供了有效平台，揭示了当前模型在复杂社会互动中的根本局限，强调需专门优化社交认知机制。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) transition from text processors to autonomous agents, evaluating their social reasoning in embodied multi-agent settings becomes critical. We introduce SocialGrid, an embodied multi-agent environment inspired by Among Us that evaluates LLM agents on planning, task execution, and social reasoning. Our evaluations reveal that even the strongest open model (GPT-OSS-120B) achieves below 60% accuracy in task completion and planning, with agents getting stuck in repetitive behaviors or failing to navigate basic obstacles. Since poor navigation confounds evaluation of social intelligence, SocialGrid offers an optional Planning Oracle to isolate social reasoning from planning deficits. While planning assistance improves task completion, social reasoning remains a bottleneck: agents fail to detect deception at near-random chance regardless of scale, relying on shallow heuristics rather than accumulating behavioral evidence. SocialGrid provides automatic failure analysis and fine-grained metrics, enabling developers to diagnose and improve their agents. We also establish a competitive leaderboard using Elo ratings from adversarial league play.

</details>


### [144] [MARCH: Multi-Agent Radiology Clinical Hierarchy for CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16175)
*Yi Lin,Yihao Ding,Yonghui Wu,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MARCH的多智能体框架，用于提升3D放射学报告生成的准确性和临床可靠性。该框架模拟放射科的专业层级结构，通过分工协作的智能体实现迭代验证和共识决策，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化3D放射学报告生成模型容易产生临床幻觉，且缺乏人类医生实践中常见的迭代验证过程。当前的视觉-语言模型多为“黑箱”系统，无法体现临床工作流中的协作监督机制。

Method: 提出MARCH（Multi-Agent Radiology Clinical Hierarchy）框架，包含三个角色：由住院医师智能体进行初步报告生成并提取多尺度CT特征，研究员智能体通过检索增强进行修订，主任医师智能体协调基于立场的迭代共识讨论以解决诊断分歧。

Result: 在RadGenome-ChestCT数据集上，MARCH在临床保真度和语言准确性方面均显著优于现有的最先进基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过模拟人类组织结构，MARCH提升了AI在高风险医学领域中的可靠性和可解释性，证明了结构化协作对医学AI系统的重要性。

Abstract: Automated 3D radiology report generation often suffers from clinical hallucinations and a lack of the iterative verification found in human practice. While recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have advanced the field, they typically operate as monolithic "black-box" systems without the collaborative oversight characteristic of clinical workflows. To address these challenges, we propose MARCH (Multi-Agent Radiology Clinical Hierarchy), a multi-agent framework that emulates the professional hierarchy of radiology departments and assigns specialized roles to distinct agents. MARCH utilizes a Resident Agent for initial drafting with multi-scale CT feature extraction, multiple Fellow Agents for retrieval-augmented revision, and an Attending Agent that orchestrates an iterative, stance-based consensus discourse to resolve diagnostic discrepancies. On the RadGenome-ChestCT dataset, MARCH significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both clinical fidelity and linguistic accuracy. Our work demonstrates that modeling human-like organizational structures enhances the reliability of AI in high-stakes medical domains.

</details>


### [145] [Characterising LLM-Generated Competency Questions: a Cross-Domain Empirical Study using Open and Closed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16258)
*Reham Alharbi,Valentina Tamma,Terry R. Payne,Jacopo de Berardinis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用生成式AI自动生成本体工程中的能力问题（CQs），提出了一套跨领域、多维度的定量评估方法，用于系统比较不同大语言模型（LLMs）生成的CQs在可读性、相关性和结构复杂性等方面的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于CQs在本体工程需求获取中的核心作用，传统依赖人工的方法效率低且参与门槛高；而生成式AI虽能大规模自动化生成CQs，但不同LLM在参数规模、领域专长和可访问性方面差异显著，亟需系统分析其生成结果的内在属性。

Method: 设计并应用一组量化指标，对来自多个开放与闭源大模型（如Llama3系列、GPT-4.1、Gemini等）在不同用例下生成的CQs进行跨域分析，评估其可读性、与输入文本的相关性及结构复杂性。

Result: 实验表明，不同LLM生成的CQs在各项指标上表现各异，且其性能受具体应用场景影响显著，呈现出由使用场景塑造的独特生成特征。

Conclusion: 生成式AI能够有效支持CQs的自动化创建，但其输出质量具有模型和场景依赖性；因此，需结合定量评估框架选择合适的LLM以适配特定本体工程需求。

Abstract: Competency Questions (CQs) are a cornerstone of requirement elicitation in ontology engineering. CQs represent requirements as a set of natural language questions that an ontology should satisfy; they are traditionally modelled by ontology engineers together with domain experts as part of a human-centred, manual elicitation process. The use of Generative AI automates CQ creation at scale, therefore democratising the process of generation, widening stakeholder engagement, and ultimately broadening access to ontology engineering. However, given the large and heterogeneous landscape of LLMs, varying in dimensions such as parameter scale, task and domain specialisation, and accessibility, it is crucial to characterise and understand the intrinsic, observable properties of the CQs they produce (e.g., readability, structural complexity) through a systematic, cross-domain analysis. This paper introduces a set of quantitative measures for the systematic comparison of CQs across multiple dimensions. Using CQs generated from well defined use cases and scenarios, we identify their salient properties, including readability, relevance with respect to the input text and structural complexity of the generated questions. We conduct our experiments over a set of use cases and requirements using a range of LLMs, including both open (KimiK2-1T, LLama3.1-8B, LLama3.2-3B) and closed models (Gemini 2.5 Pro, GPT 4.1). Our analysis demonstrates that LLM performance reflects distinct generation profiles shaped by the use case.

</details>


### [146] [Learning to Reason with Insight for Informal Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16278)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Bowen Deng,Wei Wang,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Siyang Gao,Chao Wang,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的框架DeepInsightTheorem，通过构建分层数据集和渐进式多阶段监督微调策略，提升大语言模型在非形式化定理证明中的洞察力与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非形式化定理证明中，大语言模型难以识别解决复杂数学问题所需的核心技巧，缺乏‘洞察力’成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 构建了一个名为DeepInsightTheorem的分层数据集，显式提取非形式化证明中的核心技巧和证明概要；并设计了渐进式多阶段SFT训练策略，模拟人类学习过程，逐步培养模型从书写基础证明到生成具有洞察力的解法。

Result: 在多个高难度数学基准测试上，所提方法显著优于基线模型，验证了洞察力导向的生成策略能有效提升模型的数学推理能力。

Conclusion: 通过专门训练使模型学会识别和应用核心解题技巧，可以显著增强其在非形式化定理证明中的表现，为提升LLMs的复杂推理能力提供了新路径。

Abstract: Although most of the automated theorem-proving approaches depend on formal proof systems, informal theorem proving can align better with large language models' (LLMs) strength in natural language processing. In this work, we identify a primary bottleneck in informal theorem proving as a lack of insight, namely the difficulty of recognizing the core techniques required to solve complex problems. To address this, we propose a novel framework designed to cultivate this essential reasoning skill and enable LLMs to perform insightful reasoning. We propose $\mathtt{DeepInsightTheorem}$, a hierarchical dataset that structures informal proofs by explicitly extracting core techniques and proof sketches alongside the final proof. To fully exploit this dataset, we design a Progressive Multi-Stage SFT strategy that mimics the human learning process, guiding the model from basic proof writing to insightful thinking. Our experiments on challenging mathematical benchmarks demonstrate that this insight-aware generation strategy significantly outperforms baselines. These results demonstrate that teaching models to identify and apply core techniques can substantially improve their mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [147] [Using Large Language Models and Knowledge Graphs to Improve the Interpretability of Machine Learning Models in Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16280)
*Thomas Bayer,Alexander Lohr,Sarah Weiß,Bernd Michelberger,Wolfram Höpken*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合知识图谱（KG）和大语言模型（LLM）的方法，以提升机器学习（ML）结果的可解释性，尤其在制造环境中通过XAI问题库验证了该方法在准确性、一致性、清晰度和实用性方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高机器学习模型的可解释性，使非专业用户也能理解复杂ML结果，并在实际场景中支持决策。

Method: 将领域数据与ML结果及解释存储于知识图谱中，设计选择性检索机制提取相关三元组，并由大语言模型生成用户友好的解释。

Result: 在33个XAI问题上进行评估，结果显示该方法在定量（准确性和一致性）和定性（清晰度和实用性）指标上均表现良好，尤其适用于复杂定制问题。

Conclusion: 该方法理论上有助于LLM动态访问KG以增强解释能力，实践上证明可在真实制造场景中有效支持决策。

Abstract: Explaining Machine Learning (ML) results in a transparent and user-friendly manner remains a challenging task of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI). In this paper, we present a method to enhance the interpretability of ML models by using a Knowledge Graph (KG). We store domain-specific data along with ML results and their corresponding explanations, establishing a structured connection between domain knowledge and ML insights. To make these insights accessible to users, we designed a selective retrieval method in which relevant triplets are extracted from the KG and processed by a Large Language Model (LLM) to generate user-friendly explanations of ML results. We evaluated our method in a manufacturing environment using the XAI Question Bank. Beyond standard questions, we introduce more complex, tailored questions that highlight the strengths of our approach. We evaluated 33 questions, analyzing responses using quantitative metrics such as accuracy and consistency, as well as qualitative ones such as clarity and usefulness. Our contribution is both theoretical and practical: from a theoretical perspective, we present a novel approach for effectively enabling LLMs to dynamically access a KG in order to improve the explainability of ML results. From a practical perspective, we provide empirical evidence showing that such explanations can be successfully applied in real-world manufacturing environments, supporting better decision-making in manufacturing processes.

</details>
